**[00:10:37.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000274)

**[00:10:57.280] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000276)  
Ngunit, kung sa NCR, medyo konting mas aangat yung minimum wage doon sa 488.

**[00:11:04.560] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000277)

**[00:11:13.520] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000278)

**[00:11:17.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000279)

**[00:11:27.220] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000280)

**[00:11:41.520] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000281)

**[00:11:46.400] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000282)

**[00:11:54.220] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000283)

**[00:11:58.900] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000284)

**[00:12:06.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000285)

**[00:12:11.520] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000286)

**[00:12:23.540] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000287)

**[00:12:33.940] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000288)

**[00:12:40.540] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000289)  
Sobrang konti lang yan, konting turot doon sa buong nabanggit natin ng mga programa na nasa daang-daang bilyon na.

**[00:12:50.240] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000290)  
Kaya po, dito pa lang sa tingin ko, malaki na yung problema natin kung paano ba natin matitiyak na yung mga nakatawid ay hindi babalik doon sa lubos na kahirapan at gutom.

**[00:13:02.420] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000291)

**[00:13:10.540] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000292)  
Halimbawa po, sa Naiya PPP Project, isa sa mga natagurian na fastest approved PPP proposal sa kasaysayan ng Pilipinas.

**[00:13:21.880] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000293)

**[00:14:05.380] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000297)

**[00:14:30.100] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000301)

**[00:14:33.540] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000302)

**[00:14:40.540] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000303)

**[00:14:46.320] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000304)

**[00:15:56.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000311)  
Ngayon naman po ay kaugnay ng lumalaganap na privatization at mollification ng mga palengke.

**[00:16:20.560] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000313)  
Yes.

**[00:16:21.200] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000314)  
But the particular circumstance of that, Madam Speaker, that would be under the purview of the local government unit.

**[00:16:31.340] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000315)  
Pagamat, anan dyan po ang responsibilidad ng local government units noong pagdating sa market administration,

**[00:16:38.360] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000316)  
anan dyan din naman yung role halimbawa ng PPP Center pagdating sa pagtitiyak ng mga requirements at ng mga proposals na ito, lalong-lalo na sa mga USPs.

**[00:16:51.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000317)  
Para matiyak na ito po ay hindi lang sumusunod sa mga requirements pero tumutugon din sa interest ng publiko, yung mutual benefits po ng publiko pati po ng privado pagdating sa ganitong mga proyekto.

**[00:17:08.180] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000318)  
At ang panawagan ng ating mga public markets, public and people partners, lalong-lalo na sa public markets na may mga vendors associations, retailers associations.

**[00:17:21.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000319)  
Talagang people-powered yung ating mga public markets at sila po ay handang-handa kumbaga tuminding para maisalba ang kanilang public markets.

**[00:17:31.260] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000320)  
Dahil nakita din natin, Madam Speaker, sa maraming lugar na may PPP ang kanilang mga merkado.

**[00:17:38.500] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000321)  
Lalong-lalo na pag malalaking retailers yung pumasok sa kanilang mga markets, namamatay talaga yung mga maliliit na mga manininda.

**[00:17:46.960] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000322)  
Ngayon naman, tungkol po sa monitoring ng PPP.

**[00:17:51.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000323)  
Meron po ba kayong evaluation pagdating sa mga PPP projects?

**[00:18:00.260] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000324)  
Nakikita niyo po ba halimbawa sa case ng naiya privatization na kapag tumataas po yung fees sa airport o halimbawa po nung tayo ay humarap sa oil price shock,

**[00:18:12.980] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000325)

**[00:18:21.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000326)

**[00:18:44.280] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000327)  
Hindi na po sa LGU pero nakabatay na kung sino private nila na partner.

**[00:18:50.020] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000328)  
Meron po bang monitoring?

**[00:18:51.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000329)  
Hindi na po bang monitoring at evaluation ang PPP center pagdating sa ganitong mga kaso at meron ba tayong mechanism for redress of grievances in these instances?

**[00:19:04.460] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000330)  
Madam Speaker, it would depend on the project. It's a project by project. It's a case by case basis.

**[00:19:12.580] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000331)

**[00:19:19.020] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000332)  
However, I was assured...

**[00:19:21.200] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000333)

**[00:19:34.460] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000334)  
There is a mechanism established where those who have grievances can report directly to the PPP center.

**[00:19:41.460] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000335)  
Sa ngayon po ba meron kayong mga kaso na hinahawakan tungkol dito na halimbawa ang isang partner LGU ay nais na pong putulin,

**[00:19:50.540] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000336)  
kung baga wakasan na yung kanilang kontrata na napirmahan sa panahon na hindi po nasusunod yung mga dapat na basunod na nakasaalang-alang pa rin yung interest ng publiko.

**[00:20:05.260] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000337)  
Sabi nga natin, di ba, partnership, pagdating sa mutual interest, mutual benefits, at hindi po yung isa lang ang pinakanakikinabang habang nasasakripisyo yung interest ng publiko.

**[00:20:18.220] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000338)  
Yes, Madam Speaker.

**[00:20:20.540] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000339)  
The PPP center has, it's handling cases where there are complaints with regards to privatization projects.

**[00:20:30.160] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000340)

**[00:20:48.120] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000342)  
Sa tingin ko, panahon na rin.

**[00:20:50.540] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000343)  
So, ma-repaso, ma-review ng kapulungan na ito ang ating PPP center.

**[00:20:55.640] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000344)  
So, alam ko po, relatibong bago ito pero nung naipasan natin yung bago, di ba, marami din pong mga sumulpot na mga issues, mga concerns na nasa tungkulin natin na pakinggan at bigyan ng aksyon.

**[00:21:08.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000345)

Majority Leader.

**[00:21:16.060] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-000348)

**[00:21:20.540] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-000349)  
Representative Rene Luis Co of the Kabataan Party-list for her interpellation.

**[00:21:25.040] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-000350)  
Representative Rene Luis Co of the Kabataan Party-list is now recognized for her interpellation.

**[00:21:30.780] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000351)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker, and good evening to our distinguished sponsor at 10 p.m.

**[00:21:37.660] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000352)  
Madam Speaker, my question is in relation to the Philippine Development Plan 2023 to 2028,

**[00:21:46.960] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000353)  
and we'd like to be enlightened as to...

**[00:21:50.540] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000354)  
some parts of it and even updates given that the funding that we're discussing

**[00:21:57.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000355)  
will be about the second to the last year for the implementation of the Philippine Development Plan.

**[00:22:05.320] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000356)  
Madam Speaker, within the Philippine Development Plan 2023 to 2028, I quote,

**[00:22:10.760] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000357)  
it is said that education policies have undermined better performing private schools

**[00:22:17.620] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000358)  
as seen in the declining share...

**[00:22:20.540] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000359)  
in private school enrollment, end quote.

**[00:22:24.200] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000360)  
Madam Speaker, good sponsor, what does this mean?

**[00:22:28.040] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000361)  
Is this saying na negative key performance indicator ang pagbaba ng enrollment sa private schools?

**[00:22:39.440] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000362)  
For reference, this is page 45 of the Philippine Development Plan.

**[00:22:59.500] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000363)  
Madam Speaker, if my time po...

**[00:25:40.000] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000373)  
Madam Speaker, while the Department is pulling up the justifications behind it,

**[00:25:50.540] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000374)  
Really, in terms of the declining share of private school enrollment,

**[00:25:55.060] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000375)  
it's really because this was crafted in 2022,

**[00:26:00.400] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000376)  
and during the pandemic period, a lot of private schools closed down.

**[00:26:07.780] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000377)  
So, at least on that part.

**[00:27:03.500] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000379)  
With indulgence of our distinguished colleague, the Department is now pulling up the justification behind this statement. But rest assured, at least on the part of DepDev,

**[00:27:21.000] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000380)  
this is not, it won't be an indicator of development. It is quite an [unclear] statement to make.

**[00:27:40.800] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000380-split-2)  
At least on the part of this representation, that's not really aligned also with what the Philippine Development Plan endeavors to do.

**[00:27:49.800] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000381)  
I agree, Madam Speaker.

**[00:27:52.900] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000383)  
Madam Speaker, under Article 14, Section 4 of the 1987 Constitution,

**[00:27:57.660] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000384)  
it says that the state recognizes the complementary roles of public and private institutions in the education system

**[00:28:04.800] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000385)  
and shall exercise reasonable supervision and regulation of all educational institutions.

**[00:28:11.220] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000386)  
We are alarmed by this statement, Madam Speaker,

**[00:28:13.960] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000387)  
because EDCOM 2 has noted the trend in state universities and colleges,

**[00:28:20.540] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000388)  
as well as even basic educational institutions of the increase in enrollment in public educational institutions,

**[00:28:31.860] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000389)  
especially because of the advent of the universal access to quality tertiary education.

**[00:28:37.820] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000390)  
So, to see this statement is alarming because it is saying,

**[00:28:44.560] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000391)  
and we're still continuously wondering what it's saying particularly,

**[00:28:47.540] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000392)  
is it saying na,

**[00:28:50.540] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000393)  
yung pagbaba ng kalidad ng administrasyon ay sanhi ng pagbaba ng enrollment sa private schools?

**[00:28:57.820] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000394)  
Or is it saying na ang pagbaba ng enrollment sa private schools

**[00:29:00.860] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000395)  
ay may implication din po ba ng pagbaba ng kalidad ng edukasyon?

**[00:29:06.320] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000396)  
And does that not violate the complementarity principle of private and public education institutions as provided by the Constitution?

**[00:29:15.060] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000397)  
So, can the Department submit a written explanation?

**[00:29:20.540] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000398)  
So, can the Department submit a written explanation for this statement which is in the Philippine Development Plan?

**[00:29:26.940] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000399)  
And if it would like to roll back maybe on that statement so as to not violate the complementarity principle of public and private educational institutions?

**[00:29:40.180] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000400)  
Madam Speaker?

**[00:29:41.500] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000401)  
Yes, Madam Speaker, Honorable Co.

**[00:29:44.380] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000402)  
So, that will comply, particularly with the detailed justification of why this,

**[00:29:50.540] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000403)  
ah, statement was put inside the Philippine Development Plan.

**[00:29:53.860] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000404)  
Yes.

**[00:29:54.560] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000405)  
Madam Speaker, just as a final note,

**[00:29:56.920] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000406)  
ah, it has not escaped this representation.

**[00:30:00.000] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000407)  
attention that there has been an increase in policies wherein there is an intent to roll back

**[00:30:11.280] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000408)  
support of the state to educational institutions, whether it be for pushing for public-private

**[00:30:19.720] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000409)  
partnerships in the creation of classrooms or the pushing of public-private partnerships

**[00:30:25.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000410)  
in creating commercial spaces in higher educational institutions so as they could be

**[00:30:32.840] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000411)  
fiscally autonomous and thereby, at the end of the day, just provide justification for

**[00:30:41.080] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000412)  
either cutting or lessening the budget to be allocated to them, which to this representation

**[00:30:48.180] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000413)  
might be averse to the targets provided in the PDP itself.

**[00:30:54.640] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000414)  
Under this,

**[00:30:55.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000415)  
I think page 48, it also says that government spending on education will be increased to 4-6%

**[00:31:04.440] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000416)  
of GDP in line with the Education 2030 Framework for Action. So, unfortunately, it is also this

**[00:31:13.740] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000417)  
um

**[00:31:14.300] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000418)  
the provision of the NEP that the GDP to budget ratio to education has been,

**[00:31:25.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000419)  
lessened to around 3.95%, so I'd like to ask, kamusta po ba yung paggamit natin ng target na po ito na 6% of the GDP sana yung naka-allot sa education, given that we only have 2 years left to meet the targets of PDP? Madam Speaker.

**[00:31:47.340] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000420)  
Yes, thank you Madam Speaker.

**[00:31:50.340] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000421)  
Well, at least in the Philippine Development Plan,

**[00:31:55.700] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000422)  
as has been the policy of the national government,

**[00:31:59.700] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000423)

**[00:32:02.700] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000424)  
Unfortunately,

**[00:32:04.700] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000425)

**[00:32:09.700] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000426)

**[00:32:16.700] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000427)  
for the education sector.

**[00:32:19.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000428)

**[00:32:22.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000429)  
either

**[00:32:24.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000430)

**[00:32:29.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000432)

**[00:32:33.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000434)  
of the budget

**[00:32:35.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000435)

**[00:32:37.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000436)  
to our GDP, allocated

**[00:32:39.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000437)

**[00:32:41.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000438)  
as we know, it could have went to classrooms,

**[00:32:43.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000439)

**[00:32:45.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000440)

**[00:32:49.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000442)

**[00:32:51.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000443)  
Unfortunately, what is

**[00:32:53.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000444)

**[00:32:54.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000445)

**[00:32:56.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000446)

**[00:32:58.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000447)

Madam Speaker, good sponsor.

My questions

**[00:44:10.700] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000773)

**[00:44:12.700] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000774)

**[00:44:14.700] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000775)

**[00:44:16.700] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000776)  
The proposed health

**[00:44:18.700] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000777)  
allocation is lower

**[00:44:20.700] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000778)  
than 95.7 billion

**[00:44:22.700] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000779)  
despite continuing

**[00:44:24.700] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000780)  
obligations under

**[00:44:26.700] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000781)  
RA 11223.

**[00:44:28.700] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000782)  
What explains this

**[00:44:30.700] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000783)  
decline, what criteria guided

**[00:44:32.700] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000784)  
it, and which programs and

**[00:44:34.700] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000785)  
services were reduced?

**[00:45:23.000] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000799)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker.

**[00:45:25.640] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000800)  
A couple of factors.

**[00:45:27.480] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000801)  
One is the utilization of the HFEP, particularly in 2026.

**[00:45:33.780] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000802)  
So for fiscal year 2027, we reverted back to NEP level.

**[00:45:40.080] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000803)  
Second, there is a reduction to the MYFIP.

**[00:45:43.820] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000804)  
Again, back to the NEP level.

**[00:45:46.000] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000805)  
Also, there is the factor of the one-time return of the 60 billion of PhilHealth funds,

**[00:45:51.180] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000806)  
which we cannot be sustained for the coming year.

**[00:45:56.300] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000807)  
Okay.

**[00:45:56.900] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000808)  
May I, can you please repeat the one about PhilHealth?

**[00:46:02.380] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000809)  
Which one cannot be sustained?

**[00:46:07.480] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000810)  
Oh yes, this pertains to the Supreme Court decision, Madam Speaker.

**[00:46:11.820] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000811)  
So there is a one-time return of 60 billion.

**[00:46:15.120] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000812)  
Okay, thank you, thank you, good sponsor, Madam Speaker.

**[00:46:19.080] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000813)  
How much of the reduction represents genuine deficiency savings, if at all?

**[00:46:25.480] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000814)  
And how much?

**[00:46:26.900] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000815)  
It simply means less government provision for health services.

**[00:46:45.820] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000816)  
Madam Speaker, really the reduction would not be about the efficiency rate.

**[00:46:53.200] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000817)  
It's really about the low absorptive capacity and the delays.

**[00:46:56.980] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000818)  
And the implementation.

**[00:46:59.040] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000819)  
Madam Speaker.

**[00:47:00.240] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000820)  
Thank you, Madam Sponsor, Madam Speaker.

**[00:47:04.780] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000821)  
Kapansin din po, bumaba ang alokasyon ng PhilHealth mula sa 129 billion ngayong taon

**[00:47:11.800] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000822)  
tungo sa 74 billion para sa 2027 o bawas ng 55 billion.

**[00:47:19.740] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000823)  
Bakit po kaya ito binawasan?

**[00:47:22.240] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000824)  
Gayong dapat lumalawak ang binipisyo at mas mabilis natin na,

**[00:47:26.820] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000825)  
na aabot ang mga adhikain ng universal health care.

**[00:47:31.160] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000826)  
At kasama po ba doon sa 95.7 billion na binaba, itong 55 billion na binaba sa pondo ng PhilHealth?

**[00:47:42.580] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000827)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker.

**[00:47:48.200] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000828)  
Well, pertaining to what we mentioned,

**[00:47:50.660] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000829)  
there was a 60 billion one-time return for PhilHealth Funds.

**[00:47:56.820] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000830)  
So, if you're accounting for that, actually, the 129 billion actually was just 69 billion.

**[00:48:05.060] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000831)  
Actually, nadagdagan pa po by 5 billion for this year, PhilHealth.

**[00:48:10.900] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000832)  
Okay.

**[00:48:12.200] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000833)  
Thank you, Madam Sponsor, Madam Speaker.

**[00:48:15.820] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000834)  
Dahil ang UHC ay nakabatay po sa progressive realization,

**[00:48:21.280] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000835)  
aling targets ang maaantala o hindi matutupad dahil sa nabawas na po,

**[00:48:26.820] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000836)  
sa pondo lalo sa primary care, outpatient services at catastrophic services.

**[00:48:38.400] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000837)  
Actually, Madam Speaker,

**[00:48:41.500] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000838)  
pag nagdagdagan po, kasi kung natanggal po yung 60 billion,

**[00:48:46.420] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000839)  
actually, dagdagan pa po ng 5 billion. So, actually, wala po nabawas.

**[00:48:49.520] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000840)  
Sige po. Kung atin pong igigiet and we follow that line of argument,

**[00:48:54.160] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000841)  
yung 60 billion po kasing binalik,

**[00:48:56.820] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000842)  
meron naman po talaga ng PhilHealth.

**[00:48:58.720] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000843)  
So, ibig sabihin, kung yan po ang argumento natin,

**[00:49:03.040] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000844)  
at this level, hindi pa rin nagbabago ang ibinibigay nating pondo para sa PhilHealth.

**[00:49:10.420] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000845)  
So, meaning, sa 65 billion dati na ibinigay at ngayon ay nagkakahalagan ng 74 billion

**[00:49:18.700] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000846)  
na kiniklaim po natin na nagdagdag pa ng 5 billion. Tama po?

**[00:49:24.940] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000847)  
Ayon po sa Section 37.

**[00:49:26.820] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000848)  
Ang RA 11223, malinaw na anim ang pinagkukuhanan ng pondo ng PhilHealth.

**[00:49:34.600] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000849)  
Nandiyan dyan po yung sin tax revenues, yung PAGCOR share, yung PCSO share,

**[00:49:41.460] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000850)  
yung premium contributions, yung galing sa DOH appropriations,

**[00:49:46.700] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000851)  
at national government subsidy sa GAA.

**[00:49:50.720] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000852)  
So, anim po na items.

**[00:49:52.560] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000853)  
Para sa 2027, magkano?

**[00:49:56.820] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000854)  
74 billion, paano po natin maibibreak down itong,

**[00:50:01.720] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000855)  
except of course, the premium contributions of members.

**[00:50:05.460] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000856)  
So, ano po ang mag-a-account sa 74 billion na ito?

**[00:50:11.260] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000857)  
How much is from the sin tax, from the PAGCOR, from the PCSO?

**[00:50:18.640] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000858)  
So, what does the 74 billion po represent?

**[00:50:23.480] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000859)  
So, ito, ito, ito, ito, ito.

**[00:50:26.820] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000860)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker.

**[00:50:32.520] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000861)  
55 billion will come from national government subsidies.

**[00:50:36.760] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000862)  
19 billion will come from a share of income from PAGCOR and PCSO.

**[00:50:42.680] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000863)  
So, that is the 74.

**[00:50:44.140] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000864)  
55 billion will come from?

**[00:50:46.600] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000865)  
National government subsidy.

**[00:50:48.440] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000866)  
National government. So, saan po yung sin tax?

**[00:50:50.380] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000867)  
Where is sin tax? Wala pong alokasyon dito ang sin tax?

**[00:50:56.820] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000868)  
Subsidy po ito, because this is the first time I'm hearing that the government is going to give a 55 billion subsidy to PhilHealth.

**[00:51:05.940] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000869)  
Because normally, in the past years, ang pinagagalingan po ng pondo ng PhilHealth ay sin tax lamang.

**[00:51:13.040] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000870)  
Yes, Madam Speaker. A point of clarification from the DBM.

**[00:51:16.460] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000871)  
It is, the 55 billion is coming from the sin tax.

**[00:51:19.140] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000872)  
Sin tax.

**[00:51:19.920] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000873)  
Yes.

**[00:51:20.120] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000874)  
So, 55 billion sin tax, 19 billion from PCSO and PAGCOR.

**[00:51:26.820] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000875)  
PAGCOR, is that correct?

**[00:51:27.840] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000876)  
Yes, Madam Speaker.

**[00:51:29.380] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000877)  
Itong 19 billion po ng PAGCOR, ano po ang nirepresent nito?

**[00:51:35.200] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000878)  
Siguro, tanungin ko na lamang po, simula noong 2022, magkano na po ang naibigay ng National Treasury sa PhilHealth mula sa share ng PAGCOR and PCSO?

**[00:51:51.040] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000879)  
Kasi lumalabas po sa talaan.

**[00:51:55.100] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000880)  
Madam Speaker.

**[00:51:56.820] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000881)  
I'm good sponsor.

**[00:51:57.580] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000882)  
Remember, last year, I took the floor and alam po ni Chair yun, nagkwenta po tayo kung magkano po ang utang ng gobyerno sa PhilHealth.

**[00:52:09.480] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000883)  
At lumabas po doon sa aming tala na mahigit 200 milyon ang pagkukulang ng gobyerno sa PhilHealth.

**[00:52:20.120] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000884)  
Kasama na po dyan yung mga arrears sa sin tax, PAGCOR and PCSO.

**[00:52:26.820] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000885)  
Sa talaan po ng PhilHealth ngayon, lumalabas na ang pagkukulang daw po, ang arrears daw po, ay 241 billion.

**[00:52:37.820] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000886)  
Does DBM confirm the figure? Is that also your figure? That the arrears are more or less 241 billion?

**[00:52:56.820] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000887)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker.

**[00:53:01.180] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000888)  
Well, the figure of the DBM is, the arrears would only be at 62 billion.

**[00:53:10.900] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000889)  
756,224,000.

**[00:53:14.720] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000890)  
So, dito po ibabawas yung 19 billion from the share.

**[00:53:20.300] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000891)  
I am surprised at the figure kasi last year nang nagkwentahan po kami sa floor,

**[00:53:26.820] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000892)  
galing mismo po sa figure ng DOF and DBM, nasa 200 plus billion.

**[00:53:33.800] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000893)  
Wala naman pong naidadagdag na pondo ang DBM, bakit po 62 billion na lang ito?

**[00:53:40.880] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000894)  
Nagbayad po ba or nagbigay po ba ng supplemental or additional funding ang DBM nang hindi nalalaman?

**[00:53:48.780] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000895)  
Para mabawasan ng ganito kalaking halaga yung arrears nila dapat, supposedly?

**[00:53:53.960] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000896)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker.

**[00:53:58.700] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000897)  
The 62 billion in arrears are for the PAGCOR and PCSO.

**[00:54:05.560] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000898)  
So, for the sin taxes, the updated figure would be 143,878,444,000.

**[00:54:18.980] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000899)  
Okay. Madam Speaker, good sponsor.

**[00:54:23.960] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000900)  
And can I just please request an official list from the DBM of the arrears

**[00:54:30.100] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000901)  
and to be broken down sa mga specific items po na kailangan nilang ibigay.

**[00:54:37.840] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000902)  
Yung arrears from the sin tax remittances, PAGCOR and PCSO share,

**[00:54:42.840] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000903)  
and in fact, meron dapat nga po silang babayaran na may legacy receivable po dapat ang PhilHealth sa kanila.

**[00:54:53.960] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000904)  
Yung mga premium subsidy arrears from 2016 and prior.

**[00:54:58.180] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000905)  
But anyway, gusto ko lamang pong malaman talaga, i-reconcile yung figure kung magkano po talaga ang kakulangan ng DBM

**[00:55:08.920] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000906)  
mula sa sin tax share, sa PAGCOR, sa PCSO, at yung kabuoang total po nito.

**[00:55:16.840] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000907)  
We will submit, Madam Speaker.

**[00:55:20.640] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000908)  
Yes.

**[00:55:20.900] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000909)  
So, it is established at alam naman po natin.

**[00:55:23.880] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000910)  
Kaya lahat, hindi din po ipinagkakaila na may kakulangan po talaga, ano?

**[00:55:29.560] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000911)  
Ang gusto ko pong itanong na susunod, yung kakulangan na pondo po ito,

**[00:55:33.980] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000912)  
kasi may mga halimbawa po in the past, ang collection sa sin tax sabihin nyo po ay,

**[00:55:39.620] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000913)  
I'm not sure of the complete figure, but I would just like to establish that

**[00:55:44.380] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000914)  
yung ibinibigay po ng DBM falls short sa tamang collection.

**[00:55:49.340] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000915)  
So, kaya may arrears tayo.

**[00:55:50.840] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000916)  
So, ito po bang mga perang itong hindi na ibibigay?

**[00:55:54.040] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000917)  
Nasaan na po ba ito?

**[00:55:55.860] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000918)  
Nagamit na po ba ito o nasa general fund?

**[00:56:31.000] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000924)  
Yes, Madam Speaker.

**[00:56:33.880] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000925)  
At least for the earmarked revenues, yes, these are earmarked for the UHC.

**[00:56:40.880] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000926)  
However, there isn't a mechanism in place where we can actually account for the cash that comes in for the earmarked revenue.

**[00:56:50.500] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000927)  
So, sa batas,

**[00:56:52.520] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000928)  
earmarked revenue po talaga siya,

**[00:56:53.860] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000929)  
but it can be programmed later.

**[00:56:57.180] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000930)  
Okay, I'll pursue that

**[00:56:59.520] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000931)  
a little bit later, good sponsor,

**[00:57:01.580] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000932)  
Madam Chair, no?

**[00:57:03.220] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000933)  
Kasi ang gusto ko lang

**[00:57:04.420] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000934)  
pong puntuhin diyan,

**[00:57:06.560] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000935)  
DBM na rin po nagsabi na

**[00:57:08.560] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000936)  
earmarked nga po yan.

**[00:57:10.560] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000937)  
At yung linaw po ba ng

**[00:57:12.560] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000938)  
desisyon ng Korte Suprema

**[00:57:14.400] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000939)  
nung ibinalik, pinababalik

**[00:57:16.560] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000940)  
yung 60 billion, hindi pa

**[00:57:18.580] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000941)

**[00:57:20.640] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000942)

**[00:57:22.120] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000943)  
na kung earmarked yan for

**[00:57:24.140] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000944)  
this purpose, dapat doon

**[00:57:26.560] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000945)  
lamang po siya gamitin.

**[00:57:33.860] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000946)  
Yes.

**[00:57:34.820] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000947)  
Well, under law,

**[00:57:36.240] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000948)  
if it's earmarked for that particular purpose

**[00:57:38.700] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000949)  
then it should go

**[00:57:40.100] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000950)  
to that particular purpose, which is reinforced

**[00:57:42.380] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000951)

**[00:57:43.760] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000952)  
Going along that line, good sponsor,

**[00:57:47.460] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000953)  
kasi nga po,

**[00:57:48.400] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000954)  
hindi na ibigay yung

**[00:57:49.900] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000955)

**[00:57:52.540] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000956)

**[00:57:54.220] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000957)

**[00:57:56.460] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000958)

**[00:57:58.800] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000959)

**[00:58:00.460] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000960)

**[00:58:02.560] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000961)  
So asan na po yun?

**[00:58:04.160] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000962)

**[00:58:07.620] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000963)

**[00:58:08.660] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000964)

sa PhilHealth kung sinusunod

**[00:58:14.180] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000967)

**[00:58:16.180] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000968)  
na dapat napupunta yun

**[00:58:17.920] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000969)

**[00:58:21.420] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000970)  
Madam Speaker,

as per the

**[00:58:24.400] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000972)

**[00:58:40.060] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000979)  
Yes, Madam Speaker. Thank you.

Madam Sponsor,

**[00:58:44.420] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000981)  
Madam Speaker,

Supposedly po,

Wala din pong naibibigay

So,

Madam Speaker,

mapuprotektahan ang mga direct

**[00:59:56.940] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001016)  
ng PhilHealth?

**[00:59:58.500] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001017)  
Mga ordinaryong

**[00:59:59.700] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001018)  
manggagawa po ito

**[01:00:03.300] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001019)  
na pilit na nagbabayad ng tama at sa takdang panahon. So pagdating ng punto na magkakasakit sila, kulang ang kanilang benepisyong nakukuha.

**[01:00:16.360] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001020)  
So paano po natin ito na justify at paano po natin na ipapaliwanag, maipapaliwanag ng DBM and our economic managers sa tuwing-tuwing nagbibigay po tayo ng pondo para sa universal healthcare.

**[01:01:16.360] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001022)  
Thank you Madam Speaker. Well, this representation shares the same sentiment as the Honorable Montez. As of now, there is really just a pool of social insurances.

**[01:01:33.100] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001023)  
So there isn't a differentiation as of now, the way it's treated, the fund, in terms of direct and indirect contributors.

**[01:01:43.160] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001024)  
So for the purposes of this,

**[01:01:46.360] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001025)  
the use of, and the programs of the PhilHealth, I think that would be better answered during the plenary deliberations of the Department of Health and the PhilHealth.

**[01:02:00.180] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001026)  
Yeah, but I understand Madam Sponsor, but this is about financing.

**[01:02:06.140] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001027)  
So this is about financing, which is the responsibility of the DBM and our economic managers.

**[01:02:13.560] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001028)  
It's about prioritization.

**[01:02:15.660] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001029)  
It's about our, it's about honoring our commitment to the promise of universal healthcare.

**[01:02:25.200] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001030)  
Well, anyway, ang gusto ko lamang pong ipunto na itong usaping ito ay hindi usapin ng fiscal space or absorptive capacity or programming decision lamang.

**[01:02:37.640] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001031)  
Ito ay usapin ng pagsunod sa batas at sa pangako ng gobyerno sa UHC.

**[01:02:43.260] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001032)  
Paano matutupad ang UHC?

**[01:02:45.660] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001033)  
Kung ang mismong lifeline ng PhilHealth ay napipigil.

**[01:02:50.640] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001034)  
When government appropriations for PhilHealth are withheld and arrears rich billions of pesos,

**[01:02:56.680] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001035)  
the state itself becomes a source of delayed financing.

**[01:03:01.080] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001036)  
Kung inaasa natin na magbabayad ang mga direct contributors, ang mga miyembro, sa tamang oras at tamang halaga,

**[01:03:10.540] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001037)  
government should lead by example.

**[01:03:13.040] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001038)  
Dapat manguna ang gobyerno, ang pamahalaan, sa maagap at buong pagpupondo ng universal healthcare.

**[01:03:22.920] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001039)  
Kaugnay po dito, Madam Speaker, Madam Sponsor,

**[01:03:26.040] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001040)  
On August 4, 2006, the Philippines contracted an ADB-JICA policy loan of almost $938 million or roughly $60 billion for Build Universal Healthcare Subprogram 3.

**[01:03:43.040] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001041)  
Meron 3 na po ito, so ibig sabihin may 1 and 2.

**[01:03:49.200] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001042)  
Tanong ko po sa DBM and siguro sa economic managers natin, bakit kailangan mag-utang?

**[01:03:56.760] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001043)

**[01:04:02.460] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001044)  
At saan po gagamitin itong sa Subprogram 3?

**[01:04:05.880] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001045)  
Madam Speaker, the loan?

**[01:04:13.040] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001046)  
Mentioned by the Honorable Montez is a policy loan, so it's for budgetary support.

**[01:04:18.660] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001047)  
Budgetary support for?

**[01:04:22.560] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001048)

**[01:04:26.920] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001049)

**[01:04:33.180] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001050)  
ang maaari ko po bang malaman kahit ito na lang sa Subprogram 3, saan po yun gagamitin?

**[01:04:39.960] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001051)  
Kasi ang ginamit po, universal healthcare.

**[01:04:43.040] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001052)  
So meaning, gagamitin po yan sa implementasyon ng universal healthcare.

**[01:05:12.360] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001053)  
Okay.

**[01:05:12.960] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001054)  
Ayon po sa aking pananaliksik, ang sabihin ko na lamang po na yung package 3 daw po focuses on institutionalizing reforms to ensure long-term sustainability and system-wide integration,

**[01:05:27.800] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001055)  
e-health infrastructure, automated claims processing, and resilient health system financing.

**[01:05:36.720] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001056)  
So ito pong mga ito ay dapat napupunta sa PhilHealth.

**[01:05:40.960] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001057)  
So ang tanong ko po, doon sa quinenta po natin, nagbigay po ba tayo galing sa loan na ito?

**[01:05:51.100] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001058)  
Kasi ang mga quinenta po nating nakaraan galing lang sa arrears ng sin tax, wala pa nga pong kasama ang PAGCOR at PCSO.

**[01:06:00.820] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001059)  
Ngayon po pala, meron ding policy loan. So saan napunta po yung policy loan?

**[01:06:10.960] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001060)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker.

**[01:06:20.960] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001061)  
As for the DBM, this particular policy loan goes to the general fund. So it's part of the general fund to fulfill the general fund requirements.

**[01:06:32.360] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001062)  
However, based on practice and the commitment of the national government is that this particular policy loan, so the proceeds from there,

**[01:06:40.960] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001063)  
would be attributed to programs, activities, and projects of the DOH.

**[01:06:48.400] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001064)  
Thank you, Madam Sponsor. Kaya lang naguluhan lang po ako.

**[01:06:51.600] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001065)  
Is it a normal practice that you contract a policy loan for, say, for the universal healthcare, pero hindi din mapupunta kung saan yung dapat mo purpose na inutang yun?

**[01:07:05.220] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001066)  
Is that what you're telling me, Madam Sponsor?

**[01:07:37.000] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001069)  
Madam Speaker, so

**[01:07:40.960] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001082)  
This policy loan is attributed to programs under the UHC, so not particularly just PhilHealth.

**[01:07:47.920] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001083)  
So as for the DBM, these would also include the HFEP and the MYFEP.

**[01:07:56.800] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001084)  
So also, as for the DBM, we also have different policy loans for education, disaster management,

**[01:08:05.020] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001085)  
and usually the practice is that, so the loan proceeds goes to the general fund,

**[01:08:10.400] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001086)  
but there's a commitment on the part of the national government that the proceeds from that loan will go to,

**[01:08:18.200] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001087)

**[01:08:23.840] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001088)

**[01:08:29.440] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001089)  
As for the DBM, Madam Speaker.

**[01:08:31.780] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001090)

**[01:08:35.260] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001091)

**[01:08:45.560] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001093)

**[01:08:57.760] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001094)

**[01:09:10.400] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001096)

**[01:09:12.120] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001097)

**[01:09:16.540] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001098)

**[01:09:38.520] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001102)

**[01:09:54.220] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001105)

**[01:10:09.720] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001108)

**[01:10:39.420] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001113)  
Yes, Madam.

**[01:10:39.720] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001114)  
Madam Speaker, the DBM will comply, particularly with the line-by-line details of sub-programs 1, 2,

**[01:10:46.600] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001115)

mahirap po kasing parang tanggapin na habang binabawasan natin

**[01:11:01.620] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001119)

**[01:11:09.720] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001121)

**[01:11:37.760] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001127)

**[01:11:38.920] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001128)  
Mangungutang tayo.

**[01:11:39.600] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001129)

**[01:11:46.320] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001130)  
Pero sana po sa pag-utang na iyon, katulad din ng ibang utang,

**[01:11:50.640] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001131)

**[01:11:56.580] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001132)

**[01:11:59.960] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001133)

At at the end of the period,

**[01:12:09.600] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001135)  
naubos na po natin ng pera,

**[01:12:11.700] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001136)  
masasabi natin yung inutang natin ay kapakipakinaban.

**[01:12:16.460] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001137)

**[01:12:25.860] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001138)

**[01:12:37.680] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001139)  
Sana po isa puso natin.

**[01:12:45.100] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001141)

**[01:13:39.600] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001157)  
I want to focus on how the DBM ensures that syntax revenues are properly earmarked and transparently allocated para masigurong bawat Pilipino ay may libre o abot kaya at dekalidad na health services bilang karapatan natin.

**[01:14:00.120] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001158)  
This is in continuation or in connection of what my colleague, Representative Iris Montes, have questioned earlier.

**[01:14:11.920] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001159)  
Madam Sponsor, can DBM confirm that under Republic Act No. 11223 or the Universal Health Care Act, health funding is statutory and non-discretionary?

**[01:14:27.560] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001160)  
Ibig sabihin, nakasaad.

**[01:14:30.120] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001161)  
Ibig sabihin, nasa batas at hindi basta babawasan, ihinto o ibahin ang paggagamitan.

**[01:14:38.500] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001162)  
Yes, we confirm, Madam Speaker.

**[01:14:40.520] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001163)  
Can DBM confirm that Republic Act No. 11346 and 11467 require specific percentages of all syntaxes to go directly to health programs?

**[01:14:54.820] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001164)  
Yes, Madam Speaker, we confirm.

**[01:14:56.080] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001165)  
Under Republic Act 11467.

**[01:15:00.000] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001166)  
Can DBM confirm that 60% of excise taxes from alcohol, heated tobacco, and vapor products must go strictly to universal health care?

**[01:15:13.660] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001167)  
Yes, Madam Speaker.

**[01:15:14.420] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001168)  
Can DBM also confirm that under the same law, another 20% from these same taxes is legally reserved for medical assistance for indigent patients and for upgrading public hospitals?

**[01:15:30.760] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001169)  
Yes, Madam Speaker.

**[01:15:32.400] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001170)  
Can DBM confirm that these health funds should be automatically appropriated by law?

**[01:15:39.060] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001171)  
Ibig sabihin, hindi pwede bawasan o i-divert ng Pangulo o ng sino man sa Executive?

**[01:15:48.860] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001172)  
Madam Speaker, under RA11, under the Universal Health Care Act,

**[01:15:59.520] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001173)  
there is a...

**[01:15:59.980] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001174)  
It's supposed to be earmarked.

**[01:16:02.560] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001175)  
There is no...

**[01:16:04.160] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001176)  
It's not automatic.

**[01:16:06.820] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001177)  
But it is stated and appropriated by the law.

**[01:16:10.080] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001178)  
Ibig sabihin, hindi po siya pwedeng i-divert ng Pangulo o ng sino man Executive?

**[01:16:16.760] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001179)  
Yes, Madam Speaker.

**[01:16:19.020] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001180)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Speaker, Madam Sponsor.

**[01:34:04.220] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001447)

**[01:34:22.640] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001450)

**[02:35:54.920] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002551)

**[02:36:50.180] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002562)

**[02:36:57.980] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002563)

**[02:37:04.300] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002564)

**[02:37:40.020] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002569)

**[02:37:46.800] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002570)  
Ano ang dahilan para isama natin yung para maging discontinued or

**[02:37:53.880] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002571)  
um,

**[02:37:54.280] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002572)  
para ma-discontinue yung katulad nung project na yun habang na-bid out yun.

**[02:38:02.580] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002573)  
Tapos,

**[02:38:05.940] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002574)  
short of award, na-bid out siya.

**[02:38:14.700] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002575)

**[02:38:20.100] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002576)  
well, the DBM has not, again, has not invoked the suspension.

**[02:38:24.280] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002577)

**[02:38:27.420] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002578)

**[02:38:30.760] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002579)  
the head of the agency gave the certification that it's,

**[02:38:35.220] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002580)  
it falls within these conditions.

**[02:38:39.260] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002581)

**[02:38:45.700] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002582)

**[02:38:54.280] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002583)

**[02:39:01.540] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002584)

**[02:39:10.860] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002585)

**[02:39:18.460] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002586)  
For the information of the Honorable Nazal, Madam Speaker,

**[02:39:22.860] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002587)  
the basis for that, for relying on the certification of the heads of agencies,

**[02:39:28.620] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002588)  
particularly with the discontinuance and all of that,

**[02:39:33.620] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002589)  
is that they have that mandate under EO 219 as of now.

**[02:39:43.720] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002590)  
So, that's the reason why we're relying on the certifications by the heads of agencies.

**[02:39:50.200] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002591)  
But, but I thought, as you mentioned,

**[02:39:52.860] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002592)  
earlier, hindi pa natin ini-invoke yung Section 38.

**[02:39:59.620] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002593)  
Yeah, no. This is Section 51, Chapter 9, Subtitle B, Title 1.

**[02:40:05.160] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002594)  
So, it's a different section.

**[02:40:07.060] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002595)  
This is particularly for the certification of,

**[02:40:10.720] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002596)  
by the heads of agencies regarding the final discontinuance continuation

**[02:40:15.440] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002597)  
that would revert it back to savings.

**[02:40:17.100] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002598)  
So, you're saying, Madam Chair, Madam Sponsor,

**[02:40:21.840] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002599)  
that DPWA,

**[02:40:22.860] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002600)  
with the head of the agency,

**[02:40:29.400] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002601)  
can just identify projects,

**[02:40:32.280] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002602)  
sabihin na ito ay discontinued,

**[02:40:34.940] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002603)  
at hindi na natin malalaman kung totoo ito.

**[02:40:38.180] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002604)  
And we have no way of knowing.

**[02:40:42.420] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002605)  
Tapos, ililipat nyo yung pondo sa ibang proyekto,

**[02:40:47.040] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002606)  
tapos i-augment natin kasi na-declare na natin na saving.

**[02:40:51.540] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002607)  
Aren't we already,

**[02:40:52.860] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002608)

**[02:40:57.660] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002609)  
Well, based on, well, existing, well,

**[02:41:01.800] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002610)  
existing guidelines,

**[02:41:04.540] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002611)  
and also as prescribed by EO 292, Section 51,

**[02:41:10.520] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002612)  
it would be the head of agency.

**[02:41:14.700] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002613)  
Maybe partly the rationale for that is because

**[02:41:17.040] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002614)  
they would know whether or not,

**[02:41:19.940] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002615)  
they had the technical expertise,

**[02:41:21.480] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002616)  
and they have all of the technical knowledge.

**[02:41:22.360] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002617)  
But they should,

**[02:41:22.720] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002618)  
they should talk to the director, right?

**[02:41:22.840] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002619)  
But we should have a system of checks and balances.

**[02:41:29.480] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002620)  
Because if we will say that it is not our obligation to double check,

**[02:41:35.020] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002621)  
then any agency can do this arbitrarily.

**[02:41:43.460] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002622)  
Then whatever is passed in the GAA na pinasapu natin ng Kongreso,

**[02:41:48.700] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002623)  
pwedeng palit-palitan na lang.

**[02:41:49.940] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002624)  
Yes, Madam Speaker.

**[02:41:52.900] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002625)  
We recognize the, and we also, we're one with the concerns of the Honorable Nazal.

**[02:41:59.300] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002626)  
Particularly with EO 292,

**[02:42:03.000] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002627)  
well, the DBM submits to the wisdom of Congress.

**[02:42:06.700] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002628)  
So we can amend this actually so that we put safeguards in place.

**[02:42:12.640] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002629)  
What safeguards can DBM introduce para po masigurado na hindi po mangyayari?

**[02:42:42.420] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002631)  
Well, at least on the part of the DBM,

**[02:42:47.400] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002632)  
the DBM assures this,

**[02:42:49.940] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002633)

**[02:42:58.760] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002634)

**[02:43:03.400] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002635)  
But particularly with this, the treatment of savings,

**[02:43:08.520] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002636)  
the treatment of certifications for particular projects,

**[02:43:14.720] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002637)  
the DBM submits to further amendments to the law.

**[02:43:19.940] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002638)  
As is relevant.

**[02:43:33.880] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002641)

**[02:43:37.540] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002642)

**[02:43:41.560] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002643)

**[02:43:49.940] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002645)

Thursday?

**[02:44:05.060] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002648)  
Thursday.

**[02:44:06.200] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002649)  
So tomorrow, Madam Speaker.

**[02:44:08.720] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002650)  
Thank you for that.

**[02:44:10.740] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002651)  
Sek ha, we will expect that.

**[02:44:14.520] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002652)  
And may I go to my next question, Madam Chair?

**[02:44:18.480] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002653)  
After we have everything we have just discussed

**[02:44:21.700] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002654)  
about return sa ROS savings and augmentation,

**[02:44:26.180] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002655)  
ang tanong ko po, bakit wala po sa 2027 NEP

**[02:44:29.800] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002656)  
ang chapter on overall savings?

**[02:44:33.020] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002657)  
Dati po, hanggang 2023,

**[02:44:36.200] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002658)  
for many years, the NEP showed overall savings,

**[02:44:39.740] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002659)  
how much savings were generated and where they went.

**[02:44:43.060] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002660)  
It reappeared in 2022 and 2023,

**[02:44:46.220] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002661)  
and then disappeared again.

**[02:44:47.960] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002662)  
Why?

**[02:44:48.480] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002663)  
As per the DBM, Madam Speaker,

**[02:44:52.820] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002664)  
that's not allowed, the pooling of overall savings.

**[02:44:58.760] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002665)  
Existing laws...

**[02:45:00.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002666)  
But we should have all the data available to us.

**[02:45:05.280] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002667)  
Hindi po pwedeng dapat, meron, dati nire-reflect natin sa report, tapos kung kailan lang natin gusto i-reflect, doon lang natin i-re-reflect.

**[02:45:24.260] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002668)  
So ang sinasabi ko po, dapat consistent po tayo.

**[02:45:28.160] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002669)  
So this is information that is vital to us making a decision for Congress to be able to pass the GAA.

**[02:45:38.840] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002670)  
So ano yung mga savings, yung overall savings ng lahat ng departments?

**[02:45:42.860] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002671)  
Dati nire-reflect po natin yung to and from.

**[02:45:47.160] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002672)  
We need that.

**[02:45:50.700] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002673)  
Pwede po bang ibalik?

**[02:45:58.160] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002674)  
Madam Speaker, particularly with overall savings, the establishment of overall savings, apparently that is under the Bayanihan Law, which allowed the pooling of overall savings.

**[02:46:13.700] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002675)  
But in general, that's not allowed.

**[02:46:18.160] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002676)  
Ang sinasabi, ang hinihingi lang naman po natin, yung dating nire-report natin.

**[02:46:21.480] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-002677)  
The Chair would like to remind Representative Nazal that his time has already expired.

**[02:46:24.260] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002678)  
Yes, Madam Chair.

**[02:46:25.940] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002679)  
Ang hinihingi lang, kailangan lang po natin i-clarify.

**[02:46:28.160] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002680)  
Kasi hindi pa nila nasasagot yung tanong.

**[02:46:30.820] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002681)  
So, ang hinihingi lang po natin is yung report.

**[02:46:34.940] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002682)  
Dati naman nire-report.

**[02:46:36.680] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002683)  
Pwede po ba natin ibalik?

**[02:46:38.220] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002684)  
Kasi because this is vital to us making a decision.

**[02:46:43.320] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002685)  
Bakit natin tinatago ngayon?

**[02:46:46.240] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002686)  
Pwede naman yan i-reflect sa NEP?

**[02:46:53.000] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002687)  
The DBM, Madam Speaker, will submit.

**[02:46:57.500] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002688)  
It's committed to submission.

**[02:46:58.160] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002689)  
It's committed to submit this report.

**[02:47:00.100] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002690)  
However, it's not part of the NEP.

**[02:47:03.740] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002691)  
Kaya nga po, baka pwedeng isama na sa NEP every year sa submission ng NEP.

**[02:47:12.100] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002692)  
Dati namang meron, binabalik lang natin.

**[02:47:31.360] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002694)  
Up to 2023, meron namang po tayo niyan.

**[02:47:42.500] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002695)  
Okay.

**[02:47:48.540] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002696)  
The DBM will study, but as of now, the stand is that the DBM can't create a...

**[02:47:58.160] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002697)  
chapter on overall savings.

**[02:47:59.660] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002698)  
So, we do not have that information?

**[02:48:07.480] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002699)  
The DBM will study.

**[02:48:09.040] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002700)  
Will assess.

**[02:48:10.740] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002701)  
But there is a report.

**[02:48:13.460] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002702)  
And the DBM will publish a copy to the Honorable Nazal.

**[02:48:19.140] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002703)  
That is a very simple report which was included up to 2023.

**[02:48:23.600] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002704)  
Yan yung chapter on overall savings.

**[02:48:28.160] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002705)  
So, yun lang po ang hinihingi natin na i-restore natin yun.

**[02:48:31.160] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002706)  
Ibalik natin.

**[02:48:32.740] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002707)  
Will the DBM submit to that?

**[02:48:35.980] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002708)  
This is vital information that should be shared with Congress every time.

**[02:48:47.040] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002709)  
Hindi ko maintindihan bakit kailangan natin pag-isipan pa.

**[02:48:51.980] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002710)  
Okay.

**[02:48:58.160] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002711)  
The DBM will study, yes.

**[02:49:04.240] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002712)  
Will study or comply?

**[02:49:07.940] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002713)  
No, ah, well...

**[02:49:35.500] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002714)  
Napakahira po bang i-share yung information?

**[02:49:56.400] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002715)  
Kung ano yung savings?

**[02:49:58.120] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002717)  
ng bawat ahensya at saan in-augment yung pondo na ito?

**[02:50:05.500] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002718)  
Is it that difficult?

**[02:50:07.140] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002719)  
Just to summarize and to clarify, Madam Speaker.

**[02:50:11.800] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002720)  
The reason why there was a chapter on overall savings in the 2023 NEP is because it was prescribed under Bayanihan Law.

**[02:50:22.960] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002721)  
So, that's the only reason why it was there as a chapter.

**[02:50:26.440] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002722)  
Okay.

**[02:50:27.120] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002723)  
But...

**[02:50:27.920] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002724)  
That's because it's prescribed by the Bayanihan Law.

**[02:50:30.200] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002725)  
So, that could be...

**[02:50:33.340] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002726)  
Even before Bayanihan, Madam Sponsor, meron pong reports on overall savings.

**[02:50:40.960] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002727)  
Yung to and from.

**[02:50:42.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002728)  
Kung saan, saan, saan nang galing, saan pumunta.

**[02:50:47.260] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002729)  
Meron tayong report niyan.

**[02:50:48.500] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002730)  
Hindi lang after, hindi lang during Bayanihan.

**[02:50:51.240] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002731)  
Bayanihan was 2021, ha?

**[02:50:53.980] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-002732)  
The Chair would like to respectfully remind Representative Nazal that his...

**[02:50:57.900] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-002733)  
his time has already expired to interpolate.

**[02:51:03.120] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002734)  
So, can I just ask for a report on the savings?

**[02:51:06.720] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002735)  
Yes.

**[02:51:07.500] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002736)  
Yes, Madam Speaker.

**[02:51:09.400] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002737)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[02:51:10.780] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002738)  
Thank you, Madam Sponsor.

**[02:51:12.320] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002739)

**[02:51:15.940] Floor Leader** (asr-002740)  
Madam Speaker, I move to recognize Representative JC M. Abalos for his interpellation.

**[02:51:25.210] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-002741)  
Representative JC Abalos from 4Ps Party-list

**[02:51:27.900] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-002742)  
is now recognized for his interpellation.

**[02:51:32.600] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002743)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor,

**[02:51:36.280] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002744)  
sa ahensya ng DBM at sa kanyang mga attached agency sa pangunguna ni Secretary Kim De Leon,

**[02:51:43.120] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002745)  
magandang umaga po.

**[02:51:45.820] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002746)  
Dahil inabot na po tayo ng madaling araw,

**[02:51:47.900] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002747)  
dadiretso na po ako, Madam Speaker, sa aking mga katanungan

**[02:51:50.740] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002748)  
at nakatoon po ito sa budget ng 4P's program for year 2027.

**[02:51:57.900] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002749)  
So, for the record po, Madam Speaker,

**[02:52:00.420] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002750)  
pwede po ba namin malaman kung ano ang proposed budget allocation para sa 4P's for year 2027?

**[02:52:07.680] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002751)  
At pwede po bang i-confirm ng ating sponsor na nabawasan po ang pondong ito

**[02:52:13.600] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002752)  
from around 113 billion last year to 99 billion?

**[02:52:27.900] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002753)  
Madam Speaker, for 2027, under the 2027 NEP,

**[02:52:45.820] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002754)  
the allocation would be 99 billion.

**[02:52:48.520] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002755)  
The decrease as per the DBM would be attributed to a decrease in the number of beneficiaries.

**[02:52:55.620] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002756)  
Because fortunately,

**[02:52:57.900] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002757)  
At least some of them have graduated from the four-piece program.

**[02:53:01.480] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002758)  
So, to confirm po, Madam Speaker, ayon po ba sa ating distinguished sponsor pati sa DBM,

**[02:53:06.980] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002759)  
ang primary driver sa budget decrease ay ang changes in the number of beneficiary households.

**[02:53:15.740] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002760)  
Can we get a confirmation?

**[02:53:17.880] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002761)  
Yes, Madam Speaker.

**[02:53:18.820] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002762)  
And is it true po, Madam Speaker, that for many years, government maintained a target of approximately 4.4 million households

**[02:53:26.540] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002763)  
and now we understand that the number of households is being brought down to 3.5 million.

**[02:53:35.040] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002764)  
So, gusto ko po malaman, Madam Speaker, kung ano yung empirical basis para sa 3.5 million figure.

**[02:53:40.280] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002765)  
Is it based on PSA poverty statistics, CBMS data, proxy means test, projected graduation of existing beneficiaries?

**[02:53:50.420] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002766)  
Ano po ba ang empirical basis at ang primary driver kung bakit po natin pinababa to 3.5 million?

**[02:53:56.540] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002767)  
I guess, Madam Speaker, that is correct.

**[02:54:02.040] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002768)  
So, from 4.4 million households, now it's at 3.5 million.

**[02:54:07.580] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002769)  
The 3.5 million level is from the most updated version of the listahanan, from the DSWD, Madam Speaker.

**[02:54:19.580] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002770)  
Madam Speaker, kanina nung pinapag-usapan po natin ang budget ng DepDev, binanggit ko po ang CBMS.

**[02:54:26.540] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002772)  
So, was the DBM informed na hindi na po natin ginagamit ng listahanan mula nung nagkaroon na po tayo ng CBMS?

**[02:54:36.240] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002773)  
So, sinasabi po ba ng ating distinguished sponsor na ang batayan ng 3.5 million households ay nakabatay pa rin sa listahanan na isinagawa nung nakalipas ng ilang taon na po?

**[02:54:47.700] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002774)  
Lumang datos na po yun. CBMS na po tayo ngayon, Madam Speaker.

**[02:54:50.940] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002775)  
As for the DBM, Madam Speaker,

**[02:54:56.540] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002776)  
we're still using listahanan, but now there's a feedback mechanism with the CBMS.

**[02:55:04.020] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002777)  
Once it's fully rolled out, well, it's already fully rolled out, but now we're matching between the listahanan and the...

**[02:55:15.820] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002778)  
Okay. So, Madam Speaker, para lang po malinaw, habang nandito pa po ang mga kasamahan natin mula sa DepDev,

**[02:55:21.920] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002779)  
pwede rin silang kausapin ng DBM kung gusto nila.

**[02:55:24.620] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002780)  
Sa aking pagkakaintindi, meron po tayong...

**[02:55:26.540] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002781)  
around 3 million active households under the 4-piece program.

**[02:55:30.160] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002782)  
Meron ba tayong breakdown sa 3 million na ito kung ilan dyan ang galing sa listahanan at ilan dyan ang nanggaling sa CBMS?

**[02:55:38.380] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002783)  
And could the DBM also confirm, ayon po sa DSWD mismo, may higit 100,000 households tayo subject to validation

**[02:55:46.460] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002784)  
dahil may mga pamilya na hindi mahanap sa CBMS na nasa listahanan dati.

**[02:55:52.060] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002785)  
So, pwede po ba tayong magkaroon ng kalinawan mula sa DBM?

**[02:55:55.980] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002786)  
Kung paano tayo nag-come up with a new number of household beneficiary targets while at the same time considering na hindi na po listahanan ang ginagamit ngayon, CBMS na po.

**[02:56:09.480] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002787)  
At as a matter of fact, nabanggit ko rin po kanina, 5 bilyong piso ang inilaan natin noong 2024 for the CBMS.

**[02:56:17.180] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002788)  
Sinabi rin po mismo ng DepDev kanina na kompleto na ang kanilang pag-turnover as of this year.

**[02:56:24.020] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002789)

**[02:56:25.980] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002790)

**[02:56:30.520] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002791)

**[02:56:34.120] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002792)

**[02:56:38.740] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002793)

**[02:56:43.880] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002794)

**[02:56:55.980] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002795)

**[02:57:07.920] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002796)

**[02:57:13.640] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002797)

**[02:57:20.320] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002798)

**[02:57:21.980] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002800)

**[02:57:33.320] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002801)

**[02:57:36.980] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002802)  
Considering that the CBMS Act was enacted in 2019,

**[02:57:41.080] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002803)

**[02:57:44.540] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002804)  
and we're about to allot another 5 billion peso budget for the CBMS for next year.

**[02:57:50.800] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002805)

**[02:57:51.980] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002806)

**[02:58:03.140] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002807)

**[02:58:09.400] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002808)

**[02:58:17.380] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002809)

**[02:58:21.980] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002810)

**[02:58:30.940] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002811)

**[02:58:32.900] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002812)  
At dahil po dyan, may mga pag-aaral na maaaring magkaroon ng karagdagang 1.34 billion households

**[02:58:38.580] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002813)

**[02:58:41.480] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002814)  
So gusto ko po malaman, gano'n nakatagal yung reconciliation?

**[02:58:44.700] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002815)  
Considering na we already allotted 5 billion for the CBMS in 2024,

**[02:58:50.910] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002816)  
and we're about to allot another 5 billion next year.

**[02:58:54.180] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002819)  
Yes, Madam Speaker.

**[02:58:55.980] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002820)

**[02:59:00.000] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002821)

**[03:01:56.280] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002846)

**[03:02:46.340] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002854)

**[03:03:04.360] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002857)

**[03:03:11.960] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002859)

**[03:03:14.260] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002860)

**[03:03:23.460] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002862)

**[03:03:29.260] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002863)

**[03:03:37.280] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-002864)

**[03:51:53.940] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-003901)  
and amend the same.

**[03:51:57.020] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-003902)  
Thank you.

**[03:51:57.100] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-003903)  
Is there any objection?

**[03:51:58.900] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-003904)  
The Chair hears none. Please proceed.

**[03:52:01.140] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-003905)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker.

**[03:52:02.540] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-003906)  
So, I move to terminate the period of interpellation and debate on the budget of DIPDIV and its attached agencies and corporations except the PSA.

**[03:52:21.920] Rep. Munir N. Arbison Jr.** (asr-003908)

**[03:52:37.820] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-003909)  
There is a joint motion to terminate the interpellation of the proposed budget of the Department of Economy, Planning and Development, including attached agencies and corporations, except PSA.

**[03:52:49.100] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-003910)  
Is there any objection?

**[03:52:50.820] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-003911)  
The Chair hears none.

**[03:52:51.920] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-003912)  
The Interpellations on the proposed budget of the Department of Economy, Planning and Development, including attached agencies and corporations, except the PSA, is hereby terminated.

Majority Leader.

**[03:53:09.320] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-003915)

**[03:53:10.680] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-003916)

Majority Leader.

**[03:53:18.540] Rep. Daphne A. Lagon** (asr-003918)

**[03:53:31.880] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-003921)
